# RedAlert Project X - infantry animation prompts (ChatGPT)

Generated by `make_infantry_anim_prompts.py` - edit the script, not this file.

**How to use (by hand)**

1. Start a new ChatGPT chat per sheet. **Attach the soldier's concept image** (the `Reference:` file,
   e.g. `assets/units/e1.png`) and paste the prompt.
2. Reject a sheet if the soldier changes (different gear, colours or weapon), the frame count or grid is
   wrong, figures touch each other, the camera angle changes between frames, or the background is not magenta.
3. Save it under the **Save as** name, e.g. `assets/units/e1/run-sw.png`.
4. Start with one soldier (`e1`) and test it in game before doing the rest.

**Automatic:** `python3 generate_images.py --prompts infantry-anim-prompts.md --category units --only e1/`
attaches the reference image itself.

**Directions:** moving animations are drawn in 5 directions (S, SW, W, NW, N). SE, E and NE are mirrored
from SW, W and NW in game. Still poses (stand, guard, prone) are one 3-frame `poses-<dir>` sheet per direction.

**What each sheet becomes in game**

| Sheet | Game sequence |
|---|---|
| poses-* (frames 1 / 2 / 3) | `stand` / `stand2` / `prone-stand` |
| run-* | `run` (6 frames) |
| shoot-* | `shoot` |
| dive-* | `liedown` (played backwards = `standup`) |
| crawl-* | `prone-run` |
| prone-shoot-* | `prone-shoot` |
| heal-* / repair-* | medic heal / mechanic repair |
| idle1, idle2 | `idle1`, `idle2` |
| die1, die2 | `die1`, `die2`; reused for `die3`-`die5` (die6 electro and die-crushed stay classic) |
| parachute (Rifle Infantry only) | `parachute` |


## Infantry Animations

**Rifle Infantry (`e1`)**

### 1. Rifle Infantry - idle1
Save as: `e1/idle1.png`
Reference: `assets/units/e1.png`
Grid: 4x2 frames

```
Draw exactly the same soldier as in the attached reference image (a modern rifleman with an assault rifle, helmet and body armour): same face, uniform, colours, gear, same weapon and the same pure bright green (#00FF00) team-colour patches. Sprite sheet: 8 frames laid out in a grid of 4 columns x 2 rows, read left to right then top to bottom. Idle fidget while standing still: looks left, looks right, checks the weapon / gear, adjusts the helmet, returns to the starting pose (first and last frame match). Facing south, toward the viewer. Camera: classic real-time strategy game view, orthographic, looking down at about 50 degrees, no perspective distortion; this camera never changes between frames. Every frame has exactly the same size and scale, the soldier is centred in its cell with the feet on the same ground line, with a wide clear gap of background between cells; nothing touches or crosses a cell border; the whole soldier is visible in every frame. Highly detailed realistic 3D game render, modern 2026 military gear, physically based materials, crisp and sharp. Lighting: soft sun from the top-left, identical in every frame. No cast shadow on the ground. Use no green anywhere except the team-colour patches. Isolated on a flat, solid magenta (#FF00FF) background across the whole image: no ground, no terrain, no text, no numbers, no labels, no grid lines, no border, no watermark. Wide image, aspect ratio 3:2.
```
